    <div class="comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body soft-wrap user-select-contain d-block css-overflow-wrap-anywhere">
      <p dir="auto">This is a smart way to do the counting, but one remaining issue is it doesn't really reset the counter to 0 after a test for too many same URIs. You can see that with the current setup with async/maxSameUri2, the counter begins at 1 instead of 0 due to the previous sync test. I don't have a good solution for this (other than to add yet another path param that copies the max same uri property), but would perhaps set the counter to -1 instead of 0 here to at least avoid this problem in this setup in case future tests are added.</p>
    </div>
  </task-lists>
